	option = {
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.1.a	
		#Menu 1
		if = {#Hired already
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_1			
				}
			}
			#hired already
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_hired_already_tt
			set_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already
		}
		if = {#From our province
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_1			
				}
				OR = {
				OR = {#regional
					any_owned_province = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = central_european_prostitutes	
								has_province_modifier = scandinavian_prostitutes	
							}
					}
					any_subject_country = {
						any_owned_province = {
							OR = {
								has_province_modifier = central_european_prostitutes	
								has_province_modifier = scandinavian_prostitutes	
							}
							}							
					}
				}			
				}
			}
			#hire from our madams
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_hire_our_province_tt
			set_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		if = {#From Trade
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_1			
				}
				NOT = {
				OR = {#regional
					any_owned_province = {

							}
							}							
					}
				}
				}
				OR = {
				any_province = {
					OR = {
						region = north_german_region
						region = south_german_region
						region = low_countries_region
						region = scandinavia_region
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}				
					}					
				}
			}

		}

				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_6			
				}
				OR = {#regional
					any_owned_province = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = russian_prostitutes		
							}
					}
					any_subject_country = {
						any_owned_province = {
							OR = {
								has_province_modifier = russian_prostitutes		
							}
							}							
					}
				}
			}
			#hire from our madams
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_hire_our_province_tt
			set_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		if = {#From Trade
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_6			
				}
				NOT = {
				OR = {#regional
					any_owned_province = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = russian_prostitutes		
							}
					}
					any_subject_country = {
						any_owned_province = {
							OR = {
								has_province_modifier = russian_prostitutes		
							}
							}							
					}
				}
				}
				OR = {
				any_province = {
					OR = {
							region = russia_region
							region = carpathia_region
							region = poland_region
							region = ruthenia_region
							region = baltic_region
							region = ural_region
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}				
					}					
				}
			}
			#lure with trade
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_hire_trade_tt
			set_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
		}
		if = {#From Occupation
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_6			
				}
				NOT = {
				OR = {#regional
					any_owned_province = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = russian_prostitutes		
							}
					}
					any_subject_country = {
						any_owned_province = {
							OR = {
								has_province_modifier = russian_prostitutes		
							}
							}							
					}
				}			
				}
				NOT = {
				any_province = {
					OR = {
							region = russia_region
							region = carpathia_region
							region = poland_region
							region = ruthenia_region
							region = baltic_region
							region = ural_region
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}				
					}					
				}				
				any_province = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
							region = russia_region
							region = carpathia_region
							region = poland_region
							region = ruthenia_region
							region = baltic_region
							region = ural_region
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT
					}			
			}
			#hire from occupation
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_hire_occupied_tt
			set_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		}
		if = { #Not Available
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_6			
				}
				NOT = {
				OR = {#regional
					any_owned_province = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = russian_prostitutes		
							}
					}
					any_subject_country = {
						any_owned_province = {
							OR = {
								has_province_modifier = russian_prostitutes		
							}
							}							
					}
				}			
				}
				NOT = {
				any_province = {
					OR = {
							region = russia_region
							region = carpathia_region
							region = poland_region
							region = ruthenia_region
							region = baltic_region
							region = ural_region
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}				
					}					
				}
				NOT = {
				any_province = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
							region = russia_region
							region = carpathia_region
							region = poland_region
							region = ruthenia_region
							region = baltic_region
							region = ural_region
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}

					}
				}

			#not available
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_not_available_tt
		}		
		country_event = {#open menu
			id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.106
			}																											
	}				
	option = {
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.1.10	
		country_event = {#open menu
			id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.10
			}																									
	}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.101
	title = cosmo_hiremadams_events.101.t
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.101.d1
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.101.d2
	}	
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.101.d3
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.101.d4
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { 
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			}
	}	
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.101.d5
	}		
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_EUROPE_MENU_1_eventPicture
	goto = madamprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = central_european_prostitutes	
								has_province_modifier = scandinavian_prostitutes	
							}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						region = north_german_region
						region = south_german_region
						region = low_countries_region
						region = scandinavia_region
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
						region = north_german_region
						region = south_german_region
						region = low_countries_region
						region = scandinavia_region
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.101.a	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_1

			name = brothel_europe_1
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes

			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#Hire

		}
		add_dip_power = -50
		add_years_of_income = -0.25

# Europe Menu 3
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.103
	title = cosmo_hiremadams_events.103.t
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.103.d1
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.103.d2
	}	
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.103.d3
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.103.d4
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { 
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			}
	}	
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.103.d5
	}	
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_EUROPE_MENU_3_eventPicture
	goto = madamprovince


	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = iberian_prostitutes	
							}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						region = iberia_region
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
							region = iberia_region
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.103.a	
		trigger = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_3
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_europe_3
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_europe_3
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.103.b	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
		}
		add_dip_power = -50
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_3
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_europe_3
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_europe_3
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.103.c	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		}
		add_mil_power = -25
		add_years_of_income = -0.25

		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_europe_menu_3
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_europe_3
			duration = 18250

	id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.21
	title = cosmo_hiremadams_events.21.t
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.21.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_MENA_MENU_1_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_1					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_mena_1
				}
			}
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_2					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_mena_2
				}
			}
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_3					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_mena_3
				}
			}
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_4					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_mena_4
				}
			}			
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}	
		
	
	option = {#Menu 1
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.21.a	
		if = {
			limit = {

				OR = {

					has_country_modifier = brothel_mena_1
				}
			}				
			}
			clr_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_1
			clr_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_all
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_2					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_mena_2
				}
			}				
			}
			clr_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_2
			clr_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_all
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_3					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_mena_3
				}
			}				
			}

			clr_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_all

								has_province_modifier = mena_prostitutes
							}
					OR = {
						region = mashriq_region
						region = arabia_region
					}		
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						region = mashriq_region
						region = arabia_region
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
						region = mashriq_region
						region = arabia_region
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.201.a	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_1
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_mena_1
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_mena_1
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.201.b	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
		}
		add_dip_power = -50
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_1
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_mena_1
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_mena_1
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.201.c	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		}
		add_mil_power = -25
		add_years_of_income = -0.25

		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_mena_menu_1

							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}

			}
				random_province = {

					OR = {
						region = sahel_region
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}

		}

		}

			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#Hire

# Africa Menu 3
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.303
	title = cosmo_hiremadams_events.303.t
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.303.d1
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.303.d2
	}	
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.303.d3
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.303.d4
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { 
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			}
	}	
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.303.d5
	}	
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_AFRICA_MENU_3_eventPicture
	goto = madamprovince


	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = central_african_prostitutes
							}
					OR = {
							area = south_african_plateau_area
							area = cape_of_good_hope_area
							area = quelimane_area
							}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
							area = south_african_plateau_area
							area = cape_of_good_hope_area
							area = quelimane_area
							}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
							area = south_african_plateau_area
							area = cape_of_good_hope_area
							area = quelimane_area
							}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.303.a	
		trigger = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams

			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_africa_menu_3

			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes

				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied

			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			}
	}	
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.306.d5
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_AFRICA_MENU_6_eventPicture
	goto = madamprovince


	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = east_african_prostitutes
							}
					OR = {
							region = horn_of_africa_region
					}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
							region = horn_of_africa_region
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
							region = horn_of_africa_region
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.306.a

				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}

			hidden = yes  
		}

				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}

			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.41
	title = cosmo_hiremadams_events.41.t
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.41.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_INDIA_MENU_3_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_1					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_india_1
				}
			}
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_2					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_india_2
				}
			}
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_3					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_india_3
				}
			}
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_4					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_india_4
				}
			}			
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}	
		
	
	option = {#Menu 1
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.41.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_1					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_india_1
				}
			}				
			}

			clr_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_all
		}	
		if = {

			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_2					
				}
				NOT = {

				}
			}				
			}
			clr_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_2
			clr_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_all
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_3

# India Menu 1
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.401
	title = cosmo_hiremadams_events.401.t
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.401.d1
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.401.d2
	}	
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.401.d3
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.401.d4
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { 
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			}
	}	
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.401.d5
	}		
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_INDIA_MENU_1_eventPicture
	goto = madamprovince


	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = south_asian_prostitutes
							}
					OR = {
						region = malaya_region
						region  = coromandel_region
						region  = indonesia_region
						region  = coromandel_region
						superregion  = oceania_superregion
						superregion  = far_east_superregion
					}							
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						region = coromandel_region
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
						region = coromandel_region
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.401.a	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_1
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_india_1
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_india_1
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.401.b	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
		}
		add_dip_power = -50
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_1
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_india_1
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_india_1
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.401.c	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		}
		add_mil_power = -25
		add_years_of_income = -0.25

		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_1
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_india_1
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}

							superregion = near_east_superregion
							superregion = eastern_europe_superregion
							superregion = persia_superregion
							superregion = tartary_superregion
					}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
							region = hindusthan_region
							region = west_india_region
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
							region = hindusthan_region
							region = west_india_region
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.404.a	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_4
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_india_4
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_india_4
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.404.b	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
		}
		add_dip_power = -50
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_4
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_india_4
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = {

			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned

			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.404.c	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		}
		add_mil_power = -25
		add_years_of_income = -0.25

		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_india_menu_4
		add_country_modifier = {

	}
	option = {#don't Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.404.7			
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		country_event = {#next menu
			id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.4
		}
	}

# East Indies Menu 2
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.502
	title = cosmo_hiremadams_events.502.t
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.502.d1
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.502.d2
	}	
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.502.d3
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.502.d4
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { 
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			}
	}	
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.502.d5
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_EAST_INDIES_MENU_2_eventPicture
	goto = madamprovince


	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = malay_prostitutes
							}
					OR = {
						area = north_sumatra_area
						area = central_sumatra_area
						area = south_sumatra_area
						area = indian_ocean_islands_area
						region  = coromandel_region
							}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						area = north_sumatra_area
						area = central_sumatra_area
						area = south_sumatra_area
						area = indian_ocean_islands_area
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
						area = north_sumatra_area
						area = central_sumatra_area
						area = south_sumatra_area
						area = indian_ocean_islands_area
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.502.a	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_east_indies_menu_2
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_east_indies_2
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_east_indies_2
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.502.b	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
		}
		add_dip_power = -50
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_east_indies_menu_2
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_east_indies_2
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_east_indies_2
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.502.c	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		}
		add_mil_power = -25
		add_years_of_income = -0.25

		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_east_indies_menu_2
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_east_indies_2
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_east_indies_2
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#don't Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.502.7			
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		country_event = {#next menu
			id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.5
		}
	}

		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			}
	}	
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.505.d5
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_EAST_INDIES_MENU_5_eventPicture
	goto = madamprovince


	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = malay_prostitutes
							}
					OR = {
							superregion = far_east_superregion
							area = molluca_area
							area = vogelkop_area
							area = sulawesi_area
							area = luzon_area
							area = southern_luzon_area
							area = mindanao_area
							area = west_mindanao_area
							area = visayas_area
							area = palawan_area
					}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
							area = molluca_area
							area = vogelkop_area
							area = sulawesi_area
							area = luzon_area
							area = southern_luzon_area
							area = mindanao_area
							area = west_mindanao_area
							area = visayas_area
							area = palawan_area
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
							area = molluca_area
							area = vogelkop_area
							area = sulawesi_area
							area = luzon_area
							area = southern_luzon_area
							area = mindanao_area
							area = west_mindanao_area
							area = visayas_area
							area = palawan_area
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.505.a	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_east_indies_menu_5
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_east_indies_5
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_east_indies_5
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.505.b	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
		}
		add_dip_power = -50
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_east_indies_menu_5
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_east_indies_5
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_east_indies_5
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.505.c	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		}
		add_mil_power = -25
		add_years_of_income = -0.25

		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_east_indies_menu_5
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_east_indies_5
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_east_indies_5
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#don't Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.505.7			
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		country_event = {#next menu
			id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.5
		}
	}					
}

							trade_embargoing = ROOT

				}

			clr_country_flag = brothel_china_menu_5
			clr_country_flag = brothel_china_menu_all
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_china_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_china_menu_6					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_china_6
				}
			}				
			}
			clr_country_flag = brothel_china_menu_6
			clr_country_flag = brothel_china_menu_all
		}													   																					
	}			
}

# China Menu 1
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.701
	title = cosmo_hiremadams_events.701.t
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.701.d1
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.701.d2
	}	
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.701.d3
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.701.d4
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { 
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			}
	}	
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.701.d5
	}		
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_CHINA_MENU_1_eventPicture
	goto = madamprovince


	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = chinese_prostitutes
							}
					OR = {
						superregion = europe_superregion
						superregion = north_america_superregion
						superregion = central_america_superregion
						superregion = africa_superregion
						area = taiwan_area
						area = west_guangdong_area
						area = guangdong_area
						area = fujian_area
						area = guangxi_area
					}							
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						area = taiwan_area
						area = west_guangdong_area
						area = guangdong_area
						area = fujian_area
						area = guangxi_area
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
						area = taiwan_area
						area = west_guangdong_area
						area = guangdong_area
						area = fujian_area
						area = guangxi_area
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.701.a	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_china_menu_1
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_china_1
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_china_1
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.701.b	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
		}
		add_dip_power = -50
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_china_menu_1
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_china_1
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_china_1
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.701.c	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		}
		add_mil_power = -25
		add_years_of_income = -0.25

		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_china_menu_1
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_china_1
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {

			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned

	}
	option = {#don't Hire

# China Menu 5
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.705
	title = cosmo_hiremadams_events.705.t
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.705.d1
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.705.d2
	}	
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.705.d3
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.705.d4
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { 
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			}
	}	
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.705.d5
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_CHINA_MENU_5_eventPicture
	goto = madamprovince


	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = chinese_prostitutes
							}
					OR = {
							superregion = far_east_superregion
							area = shanxi_area
							area = henan_area
							area = shandong_area
							area = hebei_area
							area = south_hebei_area
							area = henan_area
							area = north_henan_area
					}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
							area = shanxi_area
							area = henan_area
							area = shandong_area
							area = hebei_area
							area = south_hebei_area
							area = henan_area
							area = north_henan_area
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
							area = shanxi_area
							area = henan_area
							area = shandong_area
							area = hebei_area
							area = south_hebei_area
							area = henan_area
							area = north_henan_area
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.705.a	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_china_menu_5
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_china_5
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_china_5
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.705.b	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
		}
		add_dip_power = -50
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_china_menu_5
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_china_5
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_china_5
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.705.c	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		}
		add_mil_power = -25
		add_years_of_income = -0.25

		add_estate_loyalty = {

		set_country_flag = brothel_china_menu_5
		add_country_modifier = {

			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned

				}
			}

					has_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_3

				NOT = {

				}
			}
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_all

				}
				NOT = {

		}
	}

	}

		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.81.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_1					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_japan_1
				}
			}				
			}
			clr_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_1
			clr_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_all
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
			AND = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_all
					has_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_2					
				}
				NOT = {

				}
			}

			clr_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_2
			clr_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_all
		}

					has_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_3					
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_modifier = brothel_japan_3
				}
			}				
			}
			clr_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_3
			clr_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_all
		}

			AND = {

					has_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_4

# Japan Menu 1
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.801
	title = cosmo_hiremadams_events.801.t
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.801.d1
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.801.d2
	}	
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.801.d3
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.801.d4
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { 
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			}
	}	
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.801.d5
	}		
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_JAPAN_MENU_1_eventPicture
	goto = madamprovince


	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = japanese_prostitutes
							}
					OR = {
						area = shikoku_area

					}							
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
			area = shikoku_area
					}
								
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
												
					OR = {
			area = shikoku_area
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.801.a	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_1
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_japan_1
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_japan_1
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.801.b	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
		}
		add_dip_power = -50
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_1
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_japan_1
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_japan_1
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.801.c	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		}
		add_mil_power = -25
		add_years_of_income = -0.25

		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_japan_menu_1
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_japan_1
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}
		tooltip = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_japan_1
				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#don't Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.801.7			
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
		country_event = {#next menu
			id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.8
		}
	}					
}

							area = eastern_chubu_area
							area = chubu_area
							area = kanto_area
					}
								
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
												
					OR = {
							area = eastern_chubu_area
							area = chubu_area
							area = kanto_area
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.804.a

				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}

		}

			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#Hire

					}

				}
		}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_hiremadams_events.902
	title = cosmo_hiremadams_events.902.t
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.902.d1
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.902.d2
	}	
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.902.d3
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied }
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.902.d4
	}
	desc = {
	trigger = { 
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hired_already 
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied 
			}
	}	
	desc = cosmo_hiremadams_events.902.d5
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_KOREA_MENU_2_eventPicture
	goto = madamprovince


	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
								has_province_modifier = korean_prostitutes
							}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
							region = korea_region
										}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
							region = korea_region
										}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.902.a	
		trigger = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		add_years_of_income = -0.25
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_korea_menu_2
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = brothel_korea_2
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}

			add_country_modifier = {

				duration = 18250  
			}			
		}

			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied
	}
	option = {#Hire

		add_dip_power = -50

					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}												
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_trade	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
							region = korea_region
					
					}
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1			
							OR = {
						is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								is_great_power = no 
							}
						trade_share = {
							country = ROOT
							share = 25
						}								
						}					
					}
					owner = {
						NOT = {
							war_with = ROOT
							trade_embargoing = ROOT
							ai_attitude = {
								who = FROM
								attitude = attitude_hostile
							}						
						}					
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_occupied	
			}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1							
					OR = {
							region = korea_region
					
					}
					owner = {
						war_with = ROOT
					}				
					NOT = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 					
					}				
					controlled_by = ROOT				
				}
					save_event_target_as = madamprovince
				}
		}																					
	}
	}		
	
	option = {#Hire
		name = cosmo_hiremadams_events.905.a

				has_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
		}
		add_years_of_income = -0.25

			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 10
			}
		set_country_flag = brothel_korea_menu_5

			name = brothel_korea_5
			duration = 18250  
			hidden = yes  
		}

			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = brothel_korea_5

			}			
		}
			clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_hire_owned
